Only closes traces older than 1 hour to avoid\n    closing currently-running pipelines.\n    \"\"\"\n    import time as _time\n\n    tracking_uri = os.getenv(\"MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI\")\n    if not tracking_uri:\n        return\n    try:\n        import mlflow\n\n        client = mlflow.MlflowClient()\n        cutoff_ms = int((_time.time() - 3600) * 1000)  # 1 hour ago\n\n        # MLflow 3.x: search for traces with IN_PROGRESS status\n        try:\n            traces = client.search_traces(\n                experiment_ids=[\"0\"],  # Default experiment\n                filter_string=\"status = 'IN_PROGRESS'\",\n                max_results=50,\n            )\n        except (AttributeError, TypeError):\n            # search_traces may not exist or have different signature\n            logger.debug(\"MLflow search_traces API not available for orphan cleanup\")\n            return\n\n        closed = 0\n        for trace in traces:\n            trace_ts = getattr(trace.info, \"timestamp_ms\", None) or 0\n            if trace_ts < cutoff_ms:\n                try:\n                    client.end_trace(\n                        request_id=trace.info.request_id,\n                        status=\"ERROR\",\n                    )\n                    closed += 1\n                except Exception:\n                    pass  # Trace may have already been closed\n        if closed:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Startup orphan cleanup: closed %d stale IN_PROGRESS trace(s)\",\n                closed,\n            )\n    except Exception:\n        logger.debug(\"Orphan trace cleanup skipped (non-fatal)\", exc_info=True)\n\n\n# Run once at startup to clean up orphaned traces from previous crashes\n_cleanup_orphan_traces()\n\n\ndef _ensure_mlflow() -> bool:"
}

     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import time
    27→from typing import Any

    29→from api_service.storage import engine
    30→from shared.models import AuditLog
    31→from sqlmodel import Session
    32→
    33→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    34→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    35→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    36→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    37→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    38→    medgemma_decide,
    39→)
    40→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    41→
    42→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    43→
    44→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    45→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    49→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    50→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    51→    if _router is None:
    52→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    53→    return _router

    55→
    56→def _log_grounding_audit(
    57→    session: Session,
    58→    protocol_id: str,
    59→    criterion_id: str,
    60→    entity: dict[str, Any],
    61→    candidates: list[Any],
    62→    result: Any,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
    65→
    66→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
    67→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
    68→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
    69→
    70→    Args:
    71→        session: Active SQLModel session.
    72→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
    73→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
    74→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
    75→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
    76→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
    77→    """
    78→    audit = AuditLog(
    79→        event_type="entity_grounded",
    80→        target_type="criteria",
    81→        target_id=criterion_id,
    82→        details={
    83→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
    84→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
    85→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
    86→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
    87→            "candidates": [
    88→                {
    89→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
    90→                    "code": c.code,
    91→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
    92→                    "score": c.score,
    93→                }
    94→                for c in candidates
    95→            ],
    96→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
    97→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
    98→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
    99→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   100→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   101→        },
   102→    )
   103→    session.add(audit)
   104→
   105→
   106→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   107→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   108→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   109→    criterion_text: str,
   110→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   111→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   112→
   113→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   114→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   115→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   116→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   117→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   118→
   119→    Args:
   120→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   121→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   122→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   123→
   124→    Returns:
   125→        EntityGroundingResult. May have expert_review marker in reasoning.
   126→    """
   127→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   128→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   129→
   130→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   131→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   132→
   133→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   134→    if not candidates:
   135→        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   136→        if not apis:
   137→            logger.info(
   138→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   139→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   140→                entity_text[:50],
   141→                entity_type,
   142→            )
   143→        else:
   144→            logger.info(
   145→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   146→                entity_text[:50],
   147→                entity_type,
   148→            )
   149→
   150→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   151→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   152→
   153→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   154→    attempt = 1
   155→    current_query = entity_text
   156→    while (
   157→        result.selected_code is None
   158→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   159→        and attempt < 3
   160→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   161→    ):
   162→        attempt += 1
   163→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   164→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   165→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   166→            router=router,
   167→            attempt=attempt,
   168→        )
   169→
   170→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   171→            logger.info(
   172→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   173→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   174→                entity_text[:50],
   175→                attempt,
   176→            )
   177→            return EntityGroundingResult(
   178→                entity_text=entity_text,
   179→                entity_type=entity_type,
   180→                selected_code=None,
   181→                selected_system=None,
   182→                preferred_term=None,
   183→                confidence=0.0,
   184→                candidates=[],
   185→                reasoning=(
   186→                    f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   187→                    f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   188→                ),
   189→            )
   190→
   191→        new_query = (
   192→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   193→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   194→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   195→            or current_query
   196→        )
   197→        current_query = new_query
   198→
   199→        logger.info(
   200→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   201→            attempt,
   202→            entity_text[:50],
   203→            new_query[:50],
   204→        )
   205→
   206→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   207→        if new_candidates:
   208→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   209→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   210→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   211→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   212→                return EntityGroundingResult(
   213→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   214→                    entity_type=result.entity_type,
   215→                    selected_code=result.selected_code,
   216→                    selected_system=result.selected_system,
   217→                    preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   218→                    confidence=result.confidence,
   219→                    candidates=result.candidates,
   220→                    reasoning=(
   221→                        f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] {result.reasoning}"
   222→                    ),
   223→                    field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   224→                )
   225→
   226→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   227→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   228→        logger.warning(
   229→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   230→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   231→            entity_text[:50],
   232→            entity_type,
   233→            attempt,
   234→        )
   235→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   236→            entity_text=result.entity_text,
   237→            entity_type=result.entity_type,
   238→            selected_code=result.selected_code,
   239→            selected_system=result.selected_system,
   240→            preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   241→            confidence=result.confidence,
   242→            candidates=result.candidates,
   243→            reasoning=(
   244→                f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   245→                f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   246→                f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   247→            ),
   248→        )
   249→
   250→    return result
   251→
   252→
   253→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   254→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   255→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   256→    criterion_text: str,
   257→    entity_num: int,
   258→    total: int,
   259→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   260→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None]:
   261→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   262→
   263→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   264→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   265→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   266→
   267→    Args:
   268→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   269→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   270→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   271→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   272→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   273→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   274→
   275→    Returns:
   276→        (EntityGroundingResult, None) on success, or (None, error_message) on failure.
   277→    """
   278→    async with semaphore:
   279→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   280→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   281→        start = time.monotonic()
   282→        logger.info(
   283→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   284→            entity_num,
   285→            total,
   286→            entity_text,
   287→            entity_type,
   288→        )
   289→        try:
   290→            result = await _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   291→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   292→            logger.info(
   293→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, conf=%.2f",
   294→                entity_num,
   295→                total,
   296→                entity_text,
   297→                elapsed,
   298→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   299→                result.confidence,
   300→            )
   301→            return (result, None)
   302→        except Exception as e:
   303→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   304→            logger.error(
   305→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   306→                entity_num,
   307→                total,
   308→                entity_text,
   309→                elapsed,
   310→                e,
   311→            )
   312→            return (None, str(e))
   313→
   314→
   315→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   316→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   317→
   318→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   319→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   320→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   321→    entity.
   322→
   323→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   324→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   325→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   326→
   327→    Args:
   328→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   329→
   330→    Returns:
   331→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   332→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   333→    """
   334→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   335→
   336→    if state.get("error"):
   337→        return {}
   338→
   339→    with pipeline_span("ground_node", span_type="TOOL") as span:
   340→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   341→
   342→        try:
   343→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   344→            if not entities_json:
   345→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   346→                return {
   347→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   348→                }
   349→
   350→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   351→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   352→
   353→            span.set_inputs(
   354→                {
   355→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   356→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   357→                }
   358→            )
   359→
   360→            logger.info(
   361→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   362→                protocol_id,
   363→                len(entity_items),
   364→            )
   365→
   366→            router = _get_router()
   367→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   368→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   369→
   370→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   371→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   372→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   373→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   374→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   375→
   376→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   377→                    logger.info(
   378→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   379→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   380→                        entity_text[:50],
   381→                    )
   382→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   383→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   384→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   385→                        selected_code=None,
   386→                        selected_system=None,
   387→                        preferred_term=None,
   388→                        confidence=0.0,
   389→                        candidates=[],
   390→                        reasoning=(
   391→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   392→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   393→                        ),
   394→                    )
   395→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   396→                else:
   397→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   398→
   399→            # Dev/debug knob: truncate entity list for faster pipeline runs
   400→            _max_entities = int(os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES", "0"))
   401→            if _max_entities > 0:
   402→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   403→                logger.info(
   404→                    "PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   405→                    _max_entities,
   406→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   407→                )
   408→
   409→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   410→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   411→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   412→
   413→            tasks = [
   414→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   415→                    entity,
   416→                    router,
   417→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   418→                    idx,
   419→                    len(entity_items),
   420→                    semaphore,
   421→                )
   422→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   423→            ]
   424→
   425→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   426→
   427→            # Process outcomes
   428→            for (idx, entity), (result, error) in zip(entities_to_ground, outcomes):
   429→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   430→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   431→                criterion_text = entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", "")
   432→
   433→                if result is not None:
   434→                    # Generate field mappings for this entity
   435→                    field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(
   436→                        result,
   437→                        criterion_text,
   438→                    )
   439→                    result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   440→
   441→                    # Log grounding decision to AuditLog
   442→                    try:
   443→                        with Session(engine) as session:
   444→                            _log_grounding_audit(
   445→                                session,
   446→                                protocol_id,
   447→                                criterion_id,
   448→                                entity,
   449→                                result.candidates,
   450→                                result,
   451→                            )
   452→                            session.commit()
   453→                    except Exception as audit_error:
   454→                        logger.warning(
   455→                            "Failed to write AuditLog for entity '%s': %s",
   456→                            entity_text[:50],
   457→                            audit_error,
   458→                        )
   459→
   460→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   461→                else:
   462→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   463→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   464→                    error_msg = (
   465→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   466→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   467→                    )
   468→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   469→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   470→
   471→            logger.info(
   472→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   473→                protocol_id,
   474→                len(grounding_results),
   475→                len(accumulated_errors),
   476→            )
   477→
   478→            span.set_outputs(
   479→                {
   480→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   481→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   482→                }
   483→            )
   484→
   485→            return {
   486→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   487→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   488→            }
   489→
   490→        except Exception as e:
   491→            logger.exception(
   492→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   493→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   494→                e,
   495→            )
   496→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   497→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   498→

     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import json
    23→import logging
    24→from typing import Any

    26→from api_service.storage import engine
    27→from shared.models import AuditLog

    30→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    31→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState

    33→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    34→    agentic_reasoning_loop,

    36→)
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    38→
    39→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    44→
    45→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    46→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    47→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    48→    if _router is None:
    49→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    50→    return _router

    52→
    53→def _log_grounding_audit(
    54→    session: Session,
    55→    protocol_id: str,
    56→    criterion_id: str,
    57→    entity: dict[str, Any],
    58→    candidates: list[Any],
    59→    result: Any,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
    62→
    63→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
    64→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
    65→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
    66→
    67→    Args:
    68→        session: Active SQLModel session.
    69→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
    70→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
    71→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
    72→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
    73→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
    74→    """
    75→    audit = AuditLog(
    76→        event_type="entity_grounded",
    77→        target_type="criteria",
    78→        target_id=criterion_id,
    79→        details={
    80→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
    81→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
    82→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
    83→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
    84→            "candidates": [
    85→                {

    87→                    "code": c.code,

    90→                }
    91→                for c in candidates
    92→            ],

    95→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
    96→            "confidence": result.confidence,
    97→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
    98→        },
    99→    )

   102→
   103→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   104→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   105→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   106→    criterion_text: str,
   107→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   108→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   109→
   110→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   111→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   112→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   113→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   114→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   115→
   116→    Args:
   117→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   118→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   119→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   120→
   121→    Returns:
   122→        EntityGroundingResult. May have expert_review marker in reasoning.
   123→    """
   124→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   125→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   126→
   127→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   128→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   129→
   130→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   131→    if not candidates:
   132→        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   133→        if not apis:
   134→            logger.info(
   135→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   136→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   137→                entity_text[:50],
   138→                entity_type,
   139→            )
   140→        else:
   141→            logger.info(
   142→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   143→                entity_text[:50],
   144→                entity_type,
   145→            )
   146→
   147→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   148→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   149→
   150→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   151→    attempt = 1
   152→    current_query = entity_text
   153→    while (
   154→        result.selected_code is None
   155→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   156→        and attempt < 3
   157→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   158→    ):
   159→        attempt += 1
   160→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   161→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   162→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   163→            router=router,
   164→            attempt=attempt,
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   168→            logger.info(
   169→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   170→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   171→                entity_text[:50],
   172→                attempt,
   173→            )
   174→            return EntityGroundingResult(
   175→                entity_text=entity_text,
   176→                entity_type=entity_type,
   177→                selected_code=None,
   178→                selected_system=None,
   179→                preferred_term=None,
   180→                confidence=0.0,
   181→                candidates=[],
   182→                reasoning=(
   183→                    f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   184→                    f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   185→                ),
   186→            )
   187→
   188→        new_query = (
   189→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   190→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   191→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   192→            or current_query
   193→        )
   194→        current_query = new_query
   195→
   196→        logger.info(
   197→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   198→            attempt,
   199→            entity_text[:50],
   200→            new_query[:50],
   201→        )
   202→
   203→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   204→        if new_candidates:
   205→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   206→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   207→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   208→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   209→                return EntityGroundingResult(
   210→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   211→                    entity_type=result.entity_type,
   212→                    selected_code=result.selected_code,
   213→                    selected_system=result.selected_system,
   214→                    preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   215→                    confidence=result.confidence,
   216→                    candidates=result.candidates,
   217→                    reasoning=(
   218→                        f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] {result.reasoning}"
   219→                    ),
   220→                    field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   221→                )
   222→
   223→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   224→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   225→        logger.warning(
   226→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   227→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   228→            entity_text[:50],
   229→            entity_type,
   230→            attempt,
   231→        )
   232→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   233→            entity_text=result.entity_text,
   234→            entity_type=result.entity_type,
   235→            selected_code=result.selected_code,
   236→            selected_system=result.selected_system,
   237→            preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   238→            confidence=result.confidence,
   239→            candidates=result.candidates,
   240→            reasoning=(
   241→                f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   242→                f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   243→                f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   244→            ),
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    return result
   248→

   250→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   251→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   252→
   253→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   254→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   255→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   256→    entity.
   257→
   258→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   259→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total

   262→    Args:

   309→                criterion_text = entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", "")
   310→
   311→                logger.info(

   313→                    idx,

   315→                    entity_text[:50],

   317→                    criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown",
   318→                )
   319→
   320→                try:

   322→                    if entity_type == "Consent":

   328→                        result = EntityGroundingResult(

   333→                            preferred_term=None,
   334→                            confidence=0.0,
   335→                            candidates=[],

   341→                        grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())

  "content": "\"\"\"Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.\n\nThis node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:\ningest→extract→parse→ground→persist\nIt implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.\n\nPer user decisions:\n- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal\n- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog\n- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing\n- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates\n- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition\n- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)\n- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)\n- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion\n\nArchitecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom api_service.storage import engine\nfrom shared.models import AuditLog\nfrom sqlmodel import Session\n\nfrom protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult\nfrom protocol_processor.state import PipelineState\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (\n    agentic_reasoning_loop,\n    medgemma_decide,\n)\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)\n_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None\n\n\ndef _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:\n    \"\"\"Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter.\"\"\"\n    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603\n    if _router is None:\n        _router = TerminologyRouter()\n    return _router\n\n\ndef _log_grounding_audit(\n    session: Session,\n    protocol_id: str,\n    criterion_id: str,\n    entity: dict[str, Any],\n    candidates: list[Any],\n    result: Any,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.\n\n    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,\n    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:\n    \"All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged.\"\n\n    Args:\n        session: Active SQLModel session.\n        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.\n        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.\n        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.\n        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.\n        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.\n    \"\"\"\n    audit = AuditLog(\n        event_type=\"entity_grounded\",\n        target_type=\"criteria\",\n        target_id=criterion_id,\n        details={\n            \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n            \"entity_text\": result.entity_text,\n            \"entity_type\": result.entity_type,\n            \"candidate_count\": len(candidates),\n            \"candidates\": [\n                {\n                    \"source_api\": c.source_api,\n                    \"code\": c.code,\n                    \"preferred_term\": c.preferred_term,\n                    \"score\": c.score,\n                }\n                for c in candidates\n            ],\n            \"selected_code\": result.selected_code,\n            \"selected_system\": result.selected_system,\n            \"preferred_term\": result.preferred_term,\n            \"confidence\": result.confidence,\n            \"reasoning\": result.reasoning,\n        },\n    )\n    session.add(audit)\n\n\nasync def _ground_entity_with_retry(\n    entity: dict[str, Any],\n    router: TerminologyRouter,\n    criterion_text: str,\n) -> EntityGroundingResult:\n    \"\"\"Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).\n\n    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:\n    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma\n    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)\n    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)\n    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review\n\n    Args:\n        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.\n        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.\n        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.\n\n    Returns:\n        EntityGroundingResult. May have expert_review marker in reasoning.\n    \"\"\"\n    entity_text = entity.get(\"text\", \"\")\n    entity_type = entity.get(\"entity_type\", \"\")\n\n    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates\n    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)\n\n    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty\n    if not candidates:\n        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)\n        if not apis:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter\"\n                \" — no APIs configured for this type\",\n                entity_text[:50],\n                entity_type,\n            )\n        else:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs\",\n                entity_text[:50],\n                entity_type,\n            )\n\n    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates\n    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)\n\n    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total\n    attempt = 1\n    current_query = entity_text\n    while (\n        result.selected_code is None\n        and result.confidence < 0.5\n        and attempt < 3\n        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)\n    ):\n        attempt += 1\n        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(\n            entity={**entity, \"_previous_query\": current_query},\n            criterion_context=criterion_text,\n            router=router,\n            attempt=attempt,\n        )\n\n        if reasoning_result.should_skip:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning\"\n                \" (attempt %d) — skipping\",\n                entity_text[:50],\n                attempt,\n            )\n            return EntityGroundingResult(\n                entity_text=entity_text,\n                entity_type=entity_type,\n                selected_code=None,\n                selected_system=None,\n                preferred_term=None,\n                confidence=0.0,\n                candidates=[],\n                reasoning=(\n                    f\"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): \"\n                    f\"{reasoning_result.reasoning}\"\n                ),\n            )\n\n        new_query = (\n            reasoning_result.rephrased_query\n            or reasoning_result.derived_term\n            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion\n            or current_query\n        )\n        current_query = new_query\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'\",\n            attempt,\n            entity_text[:50],\n            new_query[:50],\n        )\n\n        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)\n        if new_candidates:\n            retry_entity = {**entity, \"text\": new_query}\n            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)\n            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:\n                # Success — preserve original entity_text\n                return EntityGroundingResult(\n                    entity_text=entity_text,\n                    entity_type=result.entity_type,\n                    selected_code=result.selected_code,\n                    selected_system=result.selected_system,\n                    preferred_term=result.preferred_term,\n                    confidence=result.confidence,\n                    candidates=result.candidates,\n                    reasoning=(\n                        f\"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] {result.reasoning}\"\n                    ),\n                    field_mappings=result.field_mappings,\n                )\n\n    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success\n    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:\n        logger.warning(\n            \"Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after\"\n            \" %d failed grounding attempts\",\n            entity_text[:50],\n            entity_type,\n            attempt,\n        )\n        return EntityGroundingResult(\n            entity_text=result.entity_text,\n            entity_type=result.entity_type,\n            selected_code=result.selected_code,\n            selected_system=result.selected_system,\n            preferred_term=result.preferred_term,\n            confidence=result.confidence,\n            candidates=result.candidates,\n            reasoning=(\n                f\"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after \"\n                f\"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. \"\n                f\"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n    return result\n\n\nasync def _ground_entity_parallel(\n    entity: dict[str, Any],\n    router: TerminologyRouter,\n    criterion_text: str,\n    entity_num: int,\n    total: int,\n    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,\n) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.\n\n    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.\n    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure\n    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.\n\n    Args:\n        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.\n        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.\n        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.\n        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.\n        total: Total entity count for logging.\n        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).\n\n    Returns:\n        (EntityGroundingResult, None) on success, or (None, error_message) on failure.\n    \"\"\"\n    async with semaphore:\n        entity_text = entity.get(\"text\", \"\")[:50]\n        entity_type = entity.get(\"entity_type\", \"\")\n        start = time.monotonic()\n        logger.info(\n            \"Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start\",\n            entity_num,\n            total,\n            entity_text,\n            entity_type,\n        )\n        try:\n            result = await _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)\n            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n            logger.info(\n                \"Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, conf=%.2f\",\n                entity_num,\n                total,\n                entity_text,\n                elapsed,\n                result.selected_code or \"N/A\",\n                result.confidence,\n            )\n            return (result, None)\n        except Exception as e:\n            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n            logger.error(\n                \"Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s\",\n                entity_num,\n                total,\n                entity_text,\n                elapsed,\n                e,\n            )\n            return (None, str(e))\n\n\nasync def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.\n\n    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter\n    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for\n    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded\n    entity.\n\n    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in\n    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total\n    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.\n\n    Args:\n        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,\n        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.\n    \"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span\n\n    if state.get(\"error\"):\n        return {}\n\n    with pipeline_span(\"ground_node\", span_type=\"TOOL\") as span:\n        span.set_inputs({\"protocol_id\": state.get(\"protocol_id\", \"\")})\n\n        try:\n            entities_json = state.get(\"entities_json\")\n            if not entities_json:\n                span.set_outputs({\"error\": \"No entities_json in state\"})\n                return {\n                    \"error\": \"No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed\",\n                }\n\n            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)\n            protocol_id = state[\"protocol_id\"]\n\n            span.set_inputs(\n                {\n                    \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n                    \"entity_count\": len(entity_items),\n                }\n            )\n\n            logger.info(\n                \"Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities\",\n                protocol_id,\n                len(entity_items),\n            )\n\n            router = _get_router()\n            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get(\"errors\") or [])\n            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)\n            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []\n            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):\n                entity_text = entity.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                entity_type = entity.get(\"entity_type\", \"\")\n\n                if entity_type == \"Consent\":\n                    logger.info(\n                        \"Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation\"\n                        \" criteria are not groundable to terminology codes\",\n                        entity_text[:50],\n                    )\n                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(\n                        entity_text=entity_text,\n                        entity_type=entity_type,\n                        selected_code=None,\n                        selected_system=None,\n                        preferred_term=None,\n                        confidence=0.0,\n                        candidates=[],\n                        reasoning=(\n                            \"Consent/participation criterion — not groundable\"\n                            \" to medical terminology codes\"\n                        ),\n                    )\n                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())\n                else:\n                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))\n\n            # Dev/debug knob: truncate entity list for faster pipeline runs\n            _max_entities = int(os.getenv(\"PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES\", \"0\"))\n            if _max_entities > 0:\n                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]\n                logger.info(\n                    \"PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES=%d: truncated to %d entities\",\n                    _max_entities,\n                    len(entities_to_ground),\n                )\n\n            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore\n            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop\n            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)\n\n            tasks = [\n                _ground_entity_parallel(\n                    entity,\n                    router,\n                    entity.get(\"criterion_text\") or entity.get(\"text\", \"\"),\n                    idx,\n                    len(entity_items),\n                    semaphore,\n                )\n                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground\n            ]\n\n            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n\n            # Process outcomes\n            for (idx, entity), (result, error) in zip(entities_to_ground, outcomes):\n                entity_text = entity.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                criterion_id = entity.get(\"criterion_id\", \"\")\n                criterion_text = entity.get(\"criterion_text\") or entity.get(\"text\", \"\")\n\n                if result is not None:\n                    # Generate field mappings for this entity\n                    field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(\n                        result,\n                        criterion_text,\n                    )\n                    result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None\n\n                    # Log grounding decision to AuditLog\n                    try:\n                        with Session(engine) as session:\n                            _log_grounding_audit(\n                                session,\n                                protocol_id,\n                                criterion_id,\n                                entity,\n                                result.candidates,\n                                result,\n                            )\n                            session.commit()\n                    except Exception as audit_error:\n                        logger.warning(\n                            \"Failed to write AuditLog for entity '%s': %s\",\n                            entity_text[:50],\n                            audit_error,\n                        )\n\n                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())\n                else:\n                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback\n                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else \"unknown\"\n                    error_msg = (\n                        f\"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'\"\n                        f\" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}\"\n                    )\n                    logger.error(error_msg)\n                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)\n\n            logger.info(\n                \"Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors\",\n                protocol_id,\n                len(grounding_results),\n                len(accumulated_errors),\n            )\n\n            span.set_outputs(\n                {\n                    \"grounded_count\": len(grounding_results),\n                    \"error_count\": len(accumulated_errors),\n                }\n            )\n\n            return {\n                \"grounded_entities_json\": json.dumps(grounding_results),\n                \"errors\": accumulated_errors,\n            }\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(\n                \"Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s\",\n                state.get(\"protocol_id\", \"unknown\"),\n                e,\n            )\n            span.set_outputs({\"error\": str(e)})\n            return {\"error\": f\"Ground node failed: {e}\"}\n"
}

    55→async def fetch_pdf_bytes(file_uri: str) -> bytes:
    56→    """Fetch PDF bytes from a file URI.
    57→
    58→    Supports two URI schemes:
    59→    - local:// : Reads from a local upload directory (for dev/test).
    60→      The path after 'local://' is the relative blob path within
    61→      LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR (env var, default 'uploads'). Must match the API
    62→      service's LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR so both resolve to the same protocols tree.
    63→    - gs:// : Downloads from Google Cloud Storage.
    64→
    65→    Args:
    66→        file_uri: URI of the PDF file (local:// or gs://).
    67→
    68→    Returns:
    69→        Raw PDF bytes.
    70→
    71→    Raises:
    72→        FileNotFoundError: If local file does not exist.
    73→        ValueError: If URI scheme is not recognized.
    74→    """
    75→    if file_uri.startswith("local://"):
    76→        blob_path = file_uri[len("local://") :]
    77→        upload_dir = os.environ.get("LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR", "uploads")
    78→        local_path = Path(upload_dir) / blob_path
    79→        if not local_path.exists():
    80→            raise FileNotFoundError(
    81→                f"Local PDF not found at {local_path}. "
    82→                f"Ensure LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR is set correctly "
    83→                f"(current: {upload_dir}) and the file has been uploaded."
    84→            )
    85→        logger.info("Reading PDF from local path: %s", local_path)
    86→        return local_path.read_bytes()
    87→
    88→    if file_uri.startswith("gs://"):
    89→        path_without_scheme = file_uri[len("gs://") :]

Wait — I need to be careful here. The backup had `uploads/protocols` as the default in pdf_parser.py and also in gcs.py. But the Makefile sets `LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR` to `$(pwd)/uploads`. So when that env var is set (which it is in dev), the default doesn't matter. But the gcs.py default is now `uploads` (I changed it). Let me keep them consistent — both should use `uploads/protocols` as the default to match the backup, and the Makefile override handles the actual dev path.

Actually wait, let me re-read the backup's gcs.py default:

The backup has `_LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR = Path(os.getenv("LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR", "uploads/protocols"))` — so both services default to `uploads/protocols`. But the Makefile sets it to just `uploads`. So in practice when running via Makefile, both resolve to the same `uploads` directory.

The issue was that `local_uri` included `protocols/` in the path even though `_LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR` already contained it. The backup fixed this by removing `protocols/` from the URI. Let me revert gcs.py's default to match the backup.

     1→You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Your task is to extract all inclusion and exclusion criteria from the provided clinical trial protocol document.
     2→
     3→## Instructions
     4→
     5→1. Identify ALL eligibility criteria — both inclusion and exclusion
     6→2. Extract each criterion as a separate item with its complete original text
     7→3. Classify each criterion as "inclusion" or "exclusion"
     8→4. Assign a category: medical_history, lab_values, medications, procedures, or other (NOT demographics — demographic criteria are not grounded to terminology)
     9→5. Detect temporal constraints (durations, time windows, reference points)
    10→6. Extract numeric thresholds (lab values, age ranges, dosages) — see examples below
    11→7. Identify conditional dependencies between criteria — see examples below
    12→8. Determine the assertion status for each criterion (see below)
    13→9. Assign a confidence score (0.0-1.0) based on extraction certainty
    14→10. Report the 1-based page number where each criterion appears in the PDF (page_number field)
    15→
    16→## Composite Criteria Splitting
    17→
    18→**CRITICAL: Split independent composite criteria into separate entries.**
    19→
    20→If a criterion contains INDEPENDENT conditions joined by AND, OR, or similar connectors where each condition stands alone as a distinct eligibility requirement, split them into separate criterion entries.
    21→
    22→**Split these (independent conditions — each is a distinct requirement):**
    23→- "Age 18-65 AND BMI <30" → TWO criteria: (1) "Age 18-65", (2) "BMI <30"
    24→- "Systolic BP >140 mmHg OR diastolic BP >90 mmHg" → TWO criteria
    25→- "Hemoglobin <10 g/dL AND platelet count <100,000/μL" → TWO criteria
    26→
    27→**Do NOT split these (dependent conditions — one implies or requires the other):**
    28→- "If female of childbearing potential, must have negative pregnancy test" → ONE criterion (pregnancy test depends on sex)
    29→- "Patients with diabetes who have HbA1c >9%" → ONE criterion (HbA1c condition depends on diabetes)
    30→- "Known allergy to aspirin or NSAIDs" → ONE criterion (aspirin and NSAIDs are the same category of medication)
    31→- "No history of myocardial infarction or stroke" → ONE criterion (both are cardiovascular exclusions)
    32→
    33→**Rule:** Ask "Can each condition be evaluated independently for any patient?" If YES → split. If one condition only makes sense in context of the other → keep together.
    34→
    35→## AutoCriteria Decomposition Guidance
    36→
    37→For each criterion, structure the extracted fields to capture the AutoCriteria components:
    38→- **Entity**: The medical concept (e.g., "HbA1c", "BMI", "platelet count")
    39→- **Operator**: The comparison relationship (e.g., "<", ">=", "range", "no history of")
    40→- **Value**: The threshold value (captured in numeric_thresholds)
    41→- **Unit**: The measurement unit (e.g., "g/dL", "kg/m2", "years")
    42→- **Time**: The temporal context (captured in temporal_constraint)
    43→
    44→This decomposition supports downstream UMLS grounding of the Entity component.
    45→
    46→## Numeric Thresholds and Conditions Examples
    47→
    48→Below are examples showing how to extract numeric_thresholds and conditions. Pay special attention to: age ranges use comparator "range" with upper_value, single-bound thresholds use ">=", "<=", ">", "<", or "==". For conditions: output only short phrases taken from the protocol (e.g. "if female of childbearing potential"); one phrase per conditional dependency. Do not repeat the same phrase, and do not output instruction or placeholder text like "the criterion text provided above".
    49→
    50→<EXAMPLE>
    51→<INPUT>
    52→Male or female, 40 years to 85 years old
    53→</INPUT>
    54→<OUTPUT>
    55→{
    56→  "text": "Male or female, 40 years to 85 years old",
    57→  "criteria_type": "inclusion",
    58→  "category": "other",
    59→  "numeric_thresholds": [
    60→    {
    61→      "value": 40.0,
    62→      "unit": "years",
    63→      "comparator": "range",
    64→      "upper_value": 85.0
    65→    }
    66→  ],
    67→  "temporal_constraint": null,
    68→  "conditions": [],
    69→  "assertion_status": "PRESENT",
    70→  "confidence": 1.0
    71→}
    72→</OUTPUT>
    73→</EXAMPLE>
    74→
    75→<EXAMPLE>
    76→<INPUT>
    77→HbA1c between 6.5% and 9.5%
    78→</INPUT>
    79→<OUTPUT>
    80→{
    81→  "text": "HbA1c between 6.5% and 9.5%",
    82→  "criteria_type": "inclusion",
    83→  "category": "lab_values",
    84→  "numeric_thresholds": [
    85→    {

    91→  ],
    92→  "temporal_constraint": null,
    93→  "conditions": [],
    94→  "assertion_status": "PRESENT",
    95→  "confidence": 1.0
    96→}
    97→</OUTPUT>
    98→</EXAMPLE>
    99→
   100→<EXAMPLE>
   101→<INPUT>
   102→WOMAC A pain subscale score of 1.5 or greater on a 5-point Likert scale
   103→</INPUT>
   104→<OUTPUT>
   105→{

   144→  "assertion_status": "CONDITIONAL",
   145→  "confidence": 0.9
   146→}
   147→</OUTPUT>
   148→</EXAMPLE>
   149→
   150→<EXAMPLE>
   151→<INPUT>
   152→If female of childbearing potential, must have negative pregnancy test within 7 days of enrollment
   153→</INPUT>
   154→<OUTPUT>
   155→{
   156→  "text": "If female of childbearing potential, must have negative pregnancy test within 7 days of enrollment",
   157→  "criteria_type": "inclusion",
   158→  "category": "other",
   159→  "numeric_thresholds": [
   160→    {
   161→      "value": 7.0,
   162→      "unit": "days",
   163→      "comparator": "<=",
   164→      "upper_value": null
   165→    }
   166→  ],
   167→  "temporal_constraint": {
   168→    "duration": "7 days",
   169→    "relation": "within",
   170→    "reference_point": "enrollment"
   171→  },
   172→  "conditions": ["if female of childbearing potential"],
   173→  "assertion_status": "CONDITIONAL",
   174→  "confidence": 0.9
   175→}
   176→</OUTPUT>
   177→</EXAMPLE>
   178→
   179→## Assertion Status Classification
   180→
   181→For each criterion, determine the assertion status:
   182→
   183→- **PRESENT**: The condition must be true/present for the patient.
   184→  - Example: "Age >= 18 years" → PRESENT
   185→  - Example: "Diagnosis of type 2 diabetes" → PRESENT
   186→  - Example: "ECOG performance status 0-1" → PRESENT
   187→
   188→- **ABSENT**: The condition must NOT be true/must be absent.
   189→  - Example: "No history of cardiac disease" → ABSENT
   190→  - Example: "Without prior organ transplant" → ABSENT
   191→  - Example: "Absence of active infections" → ABSENT
   192→  - Example: "No evidence of brain metastases" → ABSENT
   193→  - Example: "Free of autoimmune disorders" → ABSENT
   194→
   195→- **HYPOTHETICAL**: A theoretical or future condition.
   196→  - Example: "Willing to use contraception during study" → HYPOTHETICAL
   197→  - Example: "Able to comply with study visits" → HYPOTHETICAL
   198→  - Example: "Willing to provide informed consent" → HYPOTHETICAL
   199→
   200→- **HISTORICAL**: Refers to past medical history or prior treatment.
   201→  - Example: "Prior treatment with platinum-based chemotherapy" → HISTORICAL
   202→  - Example: "History of myocardial infarction within 6 months" → HISTORICAL
   203→  - Example: "Previous radiation therapy to the chest" → HISTORICAL
   204→
   205→- **CONDITIONAL**: Depends on another condition being true.
   206→  - Example: "If female of childbearing potential, must have negative pregnancy test" → CONDITIONAL
   207→  - Example: "For patients with diabetes, HbA1c < 8%" → CONDITIONAL
   208→  - Example: "In case of prior surgery, at least 4 weeks recovery" → CONDITIONAL
   209→
   210→## Key Negation Markers (indicate ABSENT)
   211→- "no history of", "without", "absence of", "not have", "free of", "no evidence of", "no prior", "must not", "has not", "never had"
   212→
   213→## Key Conditionality Markers (indicate CONDITIONAL)
   214→- "if", "in case of", "when applicable", "for patients who", "provided that", "unless"
   215→
   216→When populating conditions: each list entry must be a single short phrase from the protocol (the conditional part only). Do not repeat the same phrase; do not output meta-instructions or placeholders.
   217→
   218→## Output Format
   219→
   220→Return a structured extraction with:
   221→- A list of all criteria with their full structured fields
   222→- A brief protocol summary (1-2 sentences describing the trial's purpose)
   223→
   224→## Page Number Tracking
   225→
   226→For each criterion, include the `page_number` field:
   227→- Use 1-based page numbering (first page = 1)
   228→- Report the page where the criterion text begins
   229→- If a criterion spans multiple pages, use the first page
   230→- If you cannot determine the page number, set page_number to null
   231→

The three issues from your original error are all fixed:
1. **FileNotFoundError** — event now deferred to `confirm-upload` so the file exists when the processor runs
2. **MLflow "created in a different Context" warnings** — outbox now copies contextvars before thread dispatch
3. **Performance** — parallel grounding with `asyncio.gather` + `Semaphore(4)` restored
